ALASS 2.0.0 — licences of the Rust packages compiled into alass.exe

Generated by `node scripts/check-alass.mjs --write-licences` from the Cargo.lock shipped in
corresponding-source/source/Cargo.lock (SHA-256 ffd13a8055ea5129a93b5be039bb5d3f2f763e866a3eb7e64188d76b9321710a). Do not edit by hand.

71 packages are reachable from alass-cli through normal dependency edges, for any target.
Each published package’s exact source is on crates.io at the version shown; the SHA-256 is the one
Cargo.lock pins, so a downloaded crate can be checked against it. The two ALASS packages are this
release’s own source, shipped beside the executable.
